Yong Jinhu found it strange that they received special treatment even the leader of the Eunha Merchant Guild had not.

“Who are they to be treated like that? They must be incredible masters, no?”

Yong Jamyeong smiled as he said,

“They are probably more ordinary than anyone else here.”

“What?”

“If you knew what sort of person the Young Cult Leader is, it would not seem strange at all.”

Because Yong Jamyeong knew what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, he did not find such special treatment toward those two, who looked as though they had not learned even a shred of martial arts, strange at all.

Rather, he thought it was just like Geom Mugeuk.

Behind him, Geum Asu and Geum Sayeon also nodded as they looked at each other. The Young Cult Leader they had experienced had also been someone utterly special.

Before long, they passed through the main gate and entered the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Yong Jinhu’s eyes widened.

Straight ahead lay an enormous, broad avenue, wide enough for twenty carriages to pass side by side with room to spare. On either side of the road stood massive statues of evil spirits in rows.

The evil spirit statues erected like gatekeepers looked down below with terrifying, arrogant gazes.

Beyond them, endless avenues and magnificent buildings stretched out without end.

The thought came to him naturally.

‘This was made by human hands?’

The giant evil spirit statues erected everywhere were so lifelike that it seemed they might move at any moment and trample them. Simply being there truly made him feel cowed.

Not only him, but all the others visiting the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult for the first time were likewise overwhelmed by the sight within.

After walking for quite some time, they arrived at the wedding venue.

It was the largest training ground in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Seats for the guests had been prepared in front of the place where the ceremony would be held. There were seats for the invited guests, and behind them, seats where the residents of Maga Village could sit freely had also been prepared.

Outside the center, forming a semicircle, were tiers of seats where countless people could sit, like spectator seats around an arena.

Members of the Demon Army guided the invited guests to their designated seats.

Yong Jinhu was shocked to learn that his father was seated not in the first row, but the third.

‘Not even the second row, but the third?’

Once all the outside guests had taken their seats in the central area, the entrances began in earnest.

A group of warriors entered the place in orderly formation. The person at the lead was a beautiful woman dressed in elegant clothes.

Those following behind her were the Demon Swords.

“It is the Ilhwa Sword Venerate!”

At Baek Chong’s words, Yong Jinhu was startled. It was the first time he had seen the Sword Venerate he had only ever heard of.

‘So she was such a beautiful woman!’

It felt as though he were looking at a single flower blooming amid snow.

The Demon Swords took seats in one section of the spectator seats arranged on all sides, and the Ilhwa Sword Venerate came forward and sat in the second row.

‘The second row is for the Demon Venerates.’

Then Yong Jinhu found it strange.

‘Then who is the front row for?’

Just then, amid cheers, another group of warriors entered from the opposite side.

Baek Chong informed him again.

“It is the Blood Heaven Saber Demon!”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, with a great saber on his back, walked at the head, and behind him the Saber Ghosts followed in. If the Demon Swords possessed discipline, the Saber Ghosts were rough and free-spirited.

In the past, when their relationship had been poor, they would have jeered at one another, but now they did not. As the Ilhwa Sword Venerate and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had grown close, their relationship had improved as well.

The Saber Ghosts also took seats among the spectators, and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon sat beside the Ilhwa Sword Venerate.

This time, the Demon Buddha entered at the head, emitting a golden radiance. Behind him, the Mad Monks walked while chanting Buddhist prayers in unison to bless today’s wedding.

“They are demonic monks who would tear your entire body apart alive if you mistook them for mere fake monks.”

Even without Baek Chong’s warning, one could tell at a glance that they were no ordinary monks.

Today, the Demon Buddha’s radiance shone even more brightly than usual.

The next to appear were the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerate and the ghost arts masters.

When they appeared, Yong Jinhu felt dizzy.

“I suddenly feel lightheaded.”

For Yong Jinhu, whose internal energy was shallow, Baek Chong injected a strand of inner force through his back.

“It is the ghostly energy of the ghost arts masters. Breathe in deeply and exhale slowly.”

Only then did Yong Jinhu’s face relax. Even though they were not deliberately emitting their ghostly energy, it was already this much; just imagining what it would be like if they actually released it was frightening.

Next came demonic practitioners wearing splendid masks.

“They are the Faceless Guests of the Valley of Villains.”

At the words “Valley of Villains,” Yong Jinhu unconsciously averted his gaze.

The Faceless Guests were different from usual. They, who always huddled in groups of twos and threes, squatting together, were today dressed properly and walking in with dignity while wearing splendid masks. Today, they were utterly normal!

“That man must be the Extreme Evil Little Demon.”

“That is correct.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon, standing at the very front, was also wearing an ornate mask today instead of his usual white one.

Having heard plenty of the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s infamy, Yong Jinhu found himself trembling. The fear ordinary martial artists felt toward the Extreme Evil Little Demon was entirely different from what they felt toward the other Demon Venerables.

But in truth, there was no need to be so afraid today of all days.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon was smiling more brightly than ever before. He seemed overflowing with magnanimity, as though he would forgive anyone for all but the most outrageous mistake.

The Faceless Guests were the same. Since they were especially close to Geom Mugeuk, the Valley of Villains was in the midst of a festival today.

In particular, Sipchilgyo and the Crescent-Moon Faceless Guest were the happiest. Since they had not yet held their own wedding, the Young Cult Leader’s wedding today had a great influence on the two of them.

“Are their masks usually that ornate?”

At Yong Jinhu’s question, Baek Chong put the knowledge he had studied to full use.

“I heard that the Faceless Guests have many kinds of masks. Those are probably Pleasure Ghost Masks, worn for festivals or banquets.”

This time, a beautiful boy entered, leading a group of demonic martial artists.

“Look at their waists.”

They had pouches fastened at their waists. Four, five, six, seven. Each wore a different number. Only the beautiful boy at the very front wore twelve pouches.

“Don’t tell me?”

“Yes, that’s right. They are the Poison Fangs led by the Poison King. With a single gesture from them, the guests would be slaughtered in an instant.”

More than that, Yong Jinhu was shocked by the fact that the Poison King looked so young. He looked far younger than Yong Jinhu himself.

This time, a noisy clamor arose, along with the strong smell of liquor.

They were the Drunken Demon and the Drinkers. Today, the Drunken Demon had allowed the Drinkers to drink to their hearts’ content.

“What are they thinking, getting drunk already? What if they make a mistake?”

Baek Chong answered Yong Jinhu’s question.

“I heard that the drunker they become, the clearer their minds grow. They won’t make mistakes from being drunk.”

This time, the Iron Fists of the Donggwon Gate appeared in outfits that revealed their muscles.

For them, the more bare flesh they showed, the more formal and respectful it was.

The bride’s father, the Fist Demon, did not come out with them. Thus, the person standing at the front was Cheon Sohui.

In that way, all of the Eight Demon Venerables and their subordinates took their seats.

It was lively and bustling, with laughter breaking out here and there.

The guests were feasting their eyes. They were seeing people they had never seen in their lives. They were looking at the Demon Venerables, and at their elite subordinates.

They could feel anew just how precious this occasion was. When they returned, they would surely boast to everyone, exaggeration piled upon exaggeration.

Baek Chong was surprised by the atmosphere he had not expected.

He had thought it would be far heavier and darker than this.

He had expected them to use this opportunity to make it known to everyone.

We are the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult!

So he had thought the ghost art practitioners would emit ghostly energy, the Iron Fists would swing their fists, and the Poison Fangs would send up poisonous smoke.

He had thought the Faceless Guests would sneer coldly, killing intent would flow from the swords of the Demon Swords and Blade Ghosts, the Mad Monks would chant deathly prayers, and the Drinkers would make drunken scenes.

But it was bright. They were laughing and chatting. They were enjoying themselves as if they had come to a festival. So much so that they hardly felt like demonic martial artists.

It was right then.

Dudududududu.

Five carriages began to enter the place.

Not only Yong Jinhu, but all the guests were puzzled.

‘Every guest here came on foot, yet carriages are coming all the way in here? Just who on earth could they be?’

On all five large carriages approaching them, a blue wolf was painted.

Chapter 889: Let’s Swap My Son and Your Daughter
------------------------------------------------
The carriage stopped beside the place where the ceremony was being held.

"That symbol!"

There was only one place that took the blue wolf as its symbol.

Baekchong, and indeed all the guests, knew exactly where that place was.

Clack clack clack clack clack.

The doors of five carriages opened simultaneously.

From the first and fifth carriages, martial artists descended first. They were none other than the Thirteen Wolves of Sado.

Once they stood in orderly formation around the carriages, the experts of the Sado Alliance disembarked next. They were representative experts of the Sado Alliance, so renowned that merely mentioning their names was enough to know them.

They stepped down and surveyed their surroundings. Though demonic martial artists filled every direction, not one among them lost their nerve.

The one who descended next was Bisain, who overwhelmed everyone present with his imposing presence alone.

Upon seeing him, Baekchong spoke in a low voice.

"He is the Young Alliance Chief of the Sado Alliance."

It was surprising enough that the Young Alliance Chief of the Sado Alliance had come personally.

Yet another person descended from the same carriage.

"It is the Sado Alliance Chief!"

Yongjinhu was truly startled.

The Sado Alliance Chief had come in person? And accompanied by the Young Alliance Chief and their experts at that?

He could not believe it even seeing it with his own eyes. He had expected them to send a single expert as a congratulatory envoy at most.

The one who greeted them was the Eldest Young Master, Geom Muyang.

"Welcome."

"Eldest Young Master, it has been a while."

Geom Ujin and Geom Mugeuk were busy preparing for the wedding ceremony, so Geom Muyang had come to receive the guests in their stead.

"Please, come this way."

Geom Muyang guided Baek Jagang to the front row. The Demon Lords seated in the second row all rose to offer their respects with cupped-fist salutes.

"We greet the Alliance Chief."

Baek Jagang also cupped his fists and returned the greeting to the Demon Lords.

"It has been a while."

Baek Jagang and Bisain sat in the front row, while the Sado Alliance experts who had accompanied them sat with the Demon Lords in the second row.

Since they had already sat together once during the previous Three-Party Meeting, the atmosphere was not particularly awkward.

Only then did Yongjinhu understand.

'So the first row was reserved for the Sado Alliance Chief and his successor.'

Then it made sense why his father was in the third row.

It was right at that moment.

Thud thud thud thud.

There was another procession of carriages. This time, three carriages raced toward the location.

The pattern engraved on them was precisely that of the wicked spirit engraved upon the Dark Thunder Bell.

Those who descended were the elders of the Pungcheon Cult from beyond the frontier. Their eyes were shrewd and experienced, having weathered countless trials, and from them one could feel a harsh sandstorm blowing.

The last to descend was the Pungcheon Cult Leader, Sobaekta.

"It is the Pungcheon Cult Leader!"

At Baekchong's explanation, Yongjinhu looked over with a surprised expression.

"He is much younger than I thought."

This was exactly why Yongjamyeong had brought his son. When else would there be a chance to see such people? He had hoped his son would broaden his horizons by witnessing the experts of the world beyond the heavens.

As expected, Geom Muyang greeted him.

"You must have had a difficult journey, Cult Leader."

"Thank you for inviting me to this precious occasion."

Sobaekta had become far deeper in his cultivation and far more powerful compared to the past when he had fought the Phantom King alongside Geom Mugeuk, the previous Pungcheon Cult Leader, and Mabul.

Though still young, he showed no deficiency in his bearing as the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Baek Jagang, who had arrived earlier, along with the Eight Demon Lords and the Sado Alliance experts, all greeted them.

He too took a seat in the front row.

Thud thud thud thud.

This time, a single carriage arrived.

The symbol engraved on this carriage was a dragon. A magnificent dragon was depicted as if coiling around the carriage, and beneath it, characters were written.

Great Hero Heart.

Everyone could tell. They knew which faction the carriage belonged to, and whose carriage it was.

The first to descend were Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong. Jin Haryeong had not hesitated over whether to come. To say she had no lingering feelings would be a lie,

but she was not so attached that she could not come to offer congratulations. When her brother had asked, the words "Of course we must go" had spilled from her lips unbidden.

Moreover, today's congratulations were not limited to just those two.

Finally, Jinpaecheon descended from the carriage.

"It is the Murim Alliance Chief!"

Yongjinhu was startled out of his wits. He had thought the Sado Alliance or the Pungcheon Cult might attend.

But the Murim Alliance Chief coming personally? He could not believe it even seeing it with his own eyes.

It was definitely the Murim Alliance Chief. He had once greeted him together with his father long ago. Though surely the Chief could not remember a greeting from so long ago.

The guests also murmured. If no one else, they were all surprised that the Murim Alliance Chief had come in person.

The fact that Jinpaecheon had visited the main hall of the Heavenly Demon Cult with only his two grandchildren in tow was an extreme demonstration of how great his trust in Geom Mugeuk and Geom Ujin was.

In fact, the Murim Alliance's Chief Strategist, Jegal Hyeon, had advised Jinpaecheon to attend secretly in a personal capacity. If he came discreetly wearing a bamboo hat, the Heavenly Demon Cult side would surely understand.

After all, the Murim Alliance Chief officially attending the wedding of the Heavenly Demon Cult's Young Cult Lord would be a momentous event that would send massive waves through the orthodox martial arts world.

But Jinpaecheon had decided to go officially to offer his congratulations.

The fact that Geom Mugeuk had saved his grandchildren's lives alone was reason enough to attend. Moreover, if the Heavenly Demon Cult had failed to stop Cheonsalseong this time, that fiend would have swept through both the Murim Alliance and the Sado Alliance.

"If it were not for the Cult Leader and the Young Cult Lord, you and I would all be dead."

Those words were enough. Instead, he had not brought other Murim Alliance experts. He could have filled ten carriages with them if he wished, but he did not want to burden anyone.

After all, this visit was a personal one.

Geom Muyang guided Murim Alliance Chief Jinpaecheon to the front seats.

Baek Jagang and the Pungcheon Cult Leader, as well as the Eight Demon Lords and other experts, all rose to greet him.

The gazes of Jin Hagun and Bisain met.

Unconcealable delight flowed between the two.

"Today is the day."

"Yes, today is the day."

Their eyes were already brimming with mischief. That insolent Geom Mukeuk! He was the youngest among them, and yet he was getting married before them—that was simply something they could not let slide.

Today, they intended to properly display the power of the Righteous-Evil Alliance. The days of being on the receiving end would be reversed today.

Even though it was not their own wedding, their hearts pounded needlessly.

In truth, it was not just the younger ones; Jinpaecheon and Baek Jagang were in similar states of mind as well.

"You have come well."

"It is no trivial matter, so of course I had to come."

The two were now at a point where they were secretly glad to see each other. If it were not for Geom Mugeuk, theirs would have been a relationship of constant suspicion and wariness.

And so Jinpaecheon, Jin Hagun, and Jin Haryeong also sat in the front row.

One would have thought they were the last guests for the first row, but

Thud thud thud thud thud.

More carriages entered once again.

They were the most numerous to have entered thus far. A full ten carriages made their way in.

The specially treated surfaces of the carriages gave the effect of being made of ice. Added to that were snowflake patterns. They truly looked as if winter itself had arrived.

Dressed in spotless white martial robes, the experts of the North Sea Ice Palace descended. When they arrived, it felt as if the surroundings had grown chilly.

The last to descend was the North Sea Ice Palace Lord, Han Seogyeong. The Young Palace Lord Han Seol descended with her as well.

Han Seogyeong had brought the Hanbing Twin Swords and all the top experts of the North Sea Ice Palace.

Thankfully they had sent word beforehand, for the number was enough to say the North Sea Ice Palace was invading the Central Plains.

There was a reason she had gone to such lengths to bring so many.

So that Ian, alone in the Central Plains, would not be disheartened.

To show that they were her family, that her aunt had come.

After exchanging greetings with the two Alliance Chiefs and Sobaekta, Han Seogyeong smiled particularly at one person.

It was none other than Bisain, who had come all the way to the North Sea to greet her last time.

Frankly, her first impression of Bisain had not been good. His face was grotesque with scars.

But the more she spoke with him, the more she realized he was a person of true substance.

Should she call it rediscovering her daughter? Han Seogyeong thought this to herself.

'Who knew she had such unexpectedly good taste in men.'

The guests were all surprised and amazed. After the Sado Alliance Chief, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and the Murim Alliance Chief, they were now seeing the mysterious Palace Lord of the North Sea Ice Palace in person.

They felt anew both surprise and pride at the fact that they had been invited to this occasion.

Watching the Ice Palace Lord exchange greetings with the Alliance Chiefs, Yongjamyeong spoke to his son.

"Do you understand now? Why I said people more precious than your father would come."

These words flowed unbidden from Yongjinhu's mouth.

"Just what kind of person is the Young Cult Lord?"

He had shouted it out without realizing, so Yongjamyeong was startled and stopped him.

"Shh!"

But everyone around them had already heard it.

Nervous with a sense of dread, at that moment,

Jinpaecheon in the front row turned to look at him and spoke.

"Young Hero Yong."

Yongjinhu sprang to his feet and greeted him with a cupped-fist salute.

"Ah! You remember me, Alliance Chief."

"Of course I remember."

After exchanging a glance with Yongjamyeong seated beside him, Jinpaecheon spoke calmly to Yongjinhu.

"The Young Cult Lord is someone you must experience directly to understand. You cannot know him through another's words. You would never believe them otherwise."

Everyone around them smiled in agreement with those words.

"I hope that you too will have such a good opportunity in the future, Young Hero."

"Yes, Alliance Chief. I sincerely thank you for your kind words."

He had wondered if the Alliance Chief had called him to scold him for speaking so loudly, but that was not the case. The Chief had spoken out of goodwill for him.

Yongjinhu looked at his father. His father had said he wanted to introduce him to the Young Cult Lord.

'Did they really all come because of the Young Cult Lord? What kind of person is he?'

Before long, a person stepped forward before them.

The one who would preside over this wedding was the Chief Strategist, Sima Myeong.

Just from this alone, one could tell how important the Heavenly Demon Cult considered this marriage.

"Now, we shall begin the wedding ceremony."

Thunderous cheers erupted from all directions.

"The parents of the bride and groom will now enter."

Geom Ujin and Gwonma appeared from opposite sides.

At Geom Ujin's appearance, all the guests rose from their seats in unison.

Not a single breath could be heard from the spectator seats that had been noisy just moments before.

Geom Ujin slowly walked out. His presence grew more pronounced the greater the crowd.

If everyone else was black and white, he alone radiated red—such was the presence he displayed without fail even at this very moment.

Gwonma, on the other hand, appeared more nervous than ever.

He was someone who would not bat an eye even if told to fight every martial artist here, yet now he was nervous.

The two exchanged greetings and then sat in their prepared seats.

Geom Ujin exchanged glances with those in the front row.

Deeply with Jinpaecheon and Baek Jagang, lightly with Han Seogyeong and Sobaekta.

Then he exchanged voice transmissions with Gwonma.

"-In-law, how do you feel?"

"-I am bewildered. Happy, yet also somewhat sorry. Something like that."

This time, it was the other way around.

"-Cult Leader, how do you feel?"

"-I feel only joy."

"-I suppose this is the difference between the parent of a daughter and the parent of a son."

Then Geom Ujin smiled and spoke.

"Then, in-law, let us trade my son for your daughter!"

"-I refuse!"

This was the Geom Ujin who could make such casual jokes only with Gwonma.

Sima Myeong spoke in a loud voice.

"The groom shall now enter."

Boom! Doo-boom! Boom boom boom boom!

To the vigorous sound of drums, Geom Mugeuk strode in energetically, clad in ceremonial robes.

As expected, following around him in full ceremonial attire were his guards, including Jeogyeon.

Their cultivation had now reached a level where the seniors who guarded Geom Ujin would marvel.

Waaaaaaaah!

When Geom Mugeuk appeared, tremendous cheers erupted from all directions. It was not mere applause.

One could tell from those cheers how much the demonic martial artists of the Heavenly Demon Cult adored their Young Cult Lord.

They were not forced cheers, but cries of joy born of genuine affection.

And would Geom Mugeuk follow ordinary etiquette?

The Geom Mugeuk walking out grinned brightly, raising both hands in delight.

"I'm getting married!"

His words pierced through the cheers and rang clearly in the ears of every guest.

After bowing respectfully to Geom Ujin and Gwonma, Geom Mugeuk turned toward the guests.

Turning his body toward them, he looked at the people seated in the first row.

Jinpaecheon and Baek Jagang, Sobaekta and Han Seogyeong.

There were also his friends, Bisain and Jin Hagun, as well as Jin Haryeong and Han Seol.

Geom Mugeuk met each and every one of their gazes.

Geom Mugeuk knew well just how long and difficult a journey they had made.

He remembered the day he had first met them. The eyes that had doubted and distrusted him had now changed into something warm and pleasant. Amazingly, the fates of every one of them had changed after meeting Geom Mugeuk.

If he followed his heart, he would have run out to greet each and every one of them and offer his thanks. But that was something to be done at the congratulatory banquet after the ceremony was over.

Behind them, he saw the Eight Demon Venerables.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, the Poison King and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha, and even the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

Representing the Demon Venerables, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon threw a joke at Geom Mugeuk, who was smiling brightly. Since it was a formal occasion, he spoke politely.

“Young Cult Leader, are you that happy?”

“I am! I don’t think I should get drunk today. I’m so happy I feel like dancing.”

Those listening laughed in good spirits.

Then Seo Daeryong murmured softly.

“At last, there’s something I’m ahead of our Young Cult Leader in.”

It was a joke made to Dan-a and his sisters-in-law seated beside him, but Geom Mugeuk, whose ears had been transformed through rebirth, heard even that quiet remark.

“Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon, since you are my senior in married life, please teach me much from now on!”

At that, Seo Daeryong lifted high the son he was holding, Yeongha.

Laughter burst out from all around at the sight.

As befitted Geom Mugeuk’s wedding, the atmosphere was cheerful and free. He was not bound by formality.

With eyes as sharp as a hawk, Geom Mugeuk said,

“You people from Maga Village standing in the far back corner, all of you, come forward. There’s a place for you over there.”

Jang Ho led them out and had them watch from there.

A scene was unfolding that could truly be seen only at Geom Mugeuk’s wedding: the groom exchanging words with the guests, and merchants seated a little apart in a place where the Alliance Leader and the Cult Leader were present.

Now came the most important moment.

“Now, next, the bride will enter.”

This time, a beautiful melody rang out as the bride entered from the opposite side.

The martial artists of the Northern Sea Ice Palace had intended to shout even louder than the cheers that had risen when the groom entered. It was to make sure Ian would not feel intimidated. That was why as many as ten carriages had come.

But there was no need.

Waaaaaaah!

From the demonic practitioners seated among the audience, a cheer even louder than when Geom Mugeuk had appeared burst forth. Not only the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Corps, but everyone watching welcomed the bride with even greater cheers.

There, as Han Seogyeong and Han Seol looked at each other in delight, the bride of the day, dressed in splendid ceremonial robes, began to enter.

Chapter 890: Central Plains bastards, you are all beneath me!
-------------------------------------------------------------
All eyes were focused on the bride.

Ian entered wearing a splendid wedding gown, her face covered by a veil.

Behind her, women of the Cheonmajeon Guard Unit, also dressed in ceremonial attire, followed as if escorting her. Now that she was marrying the Young Leader, Cheonmajeon guards had been assigned to her as well.

Ian, walking out slowly, was nervous. So much so that she wondered if she had ever trembled like this since birth. There, ahead of her, Geom Mugeuk stood waiting for her.

No longer the Young Leader, no longer a Young Master, but as one man.

'Mugeuk.'

Ian, who had walked up slowly, stood before Geom Mugeuk. One of the women who had come out with her removed Ian's veil.

The moment her face was revealed.

"Waaaah!"

Exclamations burst from the guests. Ian, her bridal makeup applied beautifully, was truly gorgeous.

Then, a woman among the guests shouted.

"You're so beautiful!"

The one who shouted was Jin Haryeong. The Alliance Leaders, Palace Lords, and Sect Leaders sitting together in the front row all looked at her.

For her younger sister who was embarrassed by the focused attention, Jin Hagun shouted.

"The groom is handsome too!"

Geom Mugeuk and Ian both heard Jin Haryeong and Jin Hagun shouting.

Geom Mugeuk turned to Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong and smiled brightly. Since it was a place where everyone was watching, he couldn't bring himself to say, "Thank you, my friends!"

Ian was truly grateful that Jin Haryeong, of all people, had said such words for her.

Because she knew how difficult it was for her to say those words.

'Thank you, Haryeong.'

Among the guests watching that scene were Ak Gunhak and Cha Iran as well.

Geom Mugeuk had invited them to sit with the Demon Lords in the second row, but they firmly declined and sat in the back.

It was because they thought they couldn't receive the same treatment as the Demon Lords after leaving the sect.

When Geom Mugeuk and Ian had met at the Hero's Inn and started seeing each other, they hadn't known they would marry so quickly.

"Aren't you envious?"

At Cha Iran's question, Ak Gunhak looked at her with a gaze asking what she meant.

"Of them getting married."

"Isn't this question usually something a man asks a woman?"

"Since the man isn't asking, I have to do it."

Ak Gunhak, who had been looking at Cha Iran, looked back at Geom Mugeuk and Ian. Seeing Geom Mugeuk grinning from ear to ear, Ak Gunhak spoke.

"Seeing him so happy, I think I feel a bit envious."

"Shall we do it too?"

Ak Gunhak was startled by her words, which came out as if she had been waiting for this.

"I'm joking. Anyone would think you'd been ambushed. Why are you so surprised?"

Cha Iran smiled bitterly and turned her gaze toward the wedding ceremony. Then, Ak Gunhak's answer suddenly came.

"You won't regret it?"

"!"

Cha Iran looked at Ak Gunhak with a surprised face.

"They say you regret marriage whether you do it or not? My personality is the type to try first."

At her refreshing answer, a smile formed at the corners of Ak Gunhak's lips.

Cha Iran thought the conversation would just end like this, but surprising words flowed from Ak Gunhak's mouth.

"We won't be able to have it this grandly."

It was also the words she had been waiting for more than anything.

"Let's call just those two. They'll become guests worth a hundred."

The two looked at the wedding ceremony again. Cha Iran's heart began pounding as much as Ian's had when the bride entered, and from that moment on, the wedding ceremony didn't even register in her eyes.

"The bride and groom will now pay their respects to both sets of parents."

Geom Mugeuk and Ian bowed to both sets of parents.

The two paid their respects first to Geom Ujin.

"Father, I will live well."

"Father, I will live well."

Some emotion flashed across Geom Ujin's face as he looked at the two.

Geom Mugeuk could tell what that emotion was.

An emotion he had never once seen from his father.

It was none other than happiness.

'Father, were you also someone who possessed the emotion called happiness?'

What moved Geom Mugeuk even more was the fact that he saw that emotion for the first time at his own wedding.

'Father, I saw it. I saw it clearly, so from now on, I'll try to make that expression appear often.'

Geom Ujin spoke in a calm tone.

"Live well from now on."

Added to that was a whisper meant only for Ian.

"If that fellow causes you trouble, tell me."

Ian held back her tears with effort.

How could she ever repay Geom Ujin's kindness?

"Yes, then please scold him, Father."

At Ian's quiet answer, Geom Ujin smiled brightly. Father, please smile like that at your son too!

Geom Mugeuk sensed Hwi hiding behind his father. He had intentionally revealed his presence. Geom Mugeuk understood. That Hwi had conveyed his congratulations in his own way.

Geom Mugeuk looked in that direction and slightly bowed his head.

'Thank you, Uncle Hwi.'

Geom Mugeuk and Ian turned and walked toward Gwon Ma.

"Father, I will live well."

"Father, I will live well."

Seeing the two standing side by side in wedding attire, the corners of Gwon Ma's eyes turned slightly red. This was a man who wouldn't bat an eye even if the world collapsed.

"Father, I will make Ian happy."

At Geom Mugeuk's promise, Gwon Ma nodded.

Ian couldn't even look at Gwon Ma. Because if she saw his face, tears would surely fall and ruin all her makeup.

"Live happily, both of you."

"Yes, Father."

"......."

The Iron Fists of Donggwonmun all rose from their seats and roared in unison with tremendous force.

Cheon Sohui shouted as if leading the chorus.

"Senior Brother, be happy!"

The Iron Fists all shouted together. At least for this moment, he was not the Young Leader, but a senior brother who had once trained together, sweating side by side.

And they were proud that Geom Mugeuk was their senior brother.

"Senior Brother, be happy!"

Among the guests watching that sight were Cheongmyeon and Seo Jin as well.

"Squad Leader Seo."

"Yes, First Squad Leader."

"Take on the position of Ghost Shadow Leader from now on."

At those words, Seo Jin was greatly surprised.

Ian had entrusted the position of Ghost Shadow Leader to Cheongmyeon upon her marriage. Yet now he was handing that position over to Seo Jin.

"I have already spoken to the Great Leader and received permission."

"Why so suddenly?"

"I am returning to the Valley of Evil."

Cheongmyeon had decided to return under the Geugak Soma.

"The Soma has agreed to accept me."

When they had met while fighting against the forces behind the scenes, the Geugak Soma had told him that he could return if he wished. His heart had been half-inclined, but Ian stepping down from the Ghost Shadow Leader position served as the occasion for him to make up his mind to return.

Half the reason he had taken the position of First Squad Leader of the Ghost Shadow Division was because of Geom Mugeuk, and half was because of Ian. Now that half was gone, he had resolved to return.

"Can I really take on such a heavy responsibility?"

"You can. And from the beginning, the Great Leader was thinking of making Squad Leader Seo the Leader if it wasn't me."

It was the moment Seo Jin became the Ghost Shadow Leader.

It was the Ghost Shadow Division that would continue to serve as Geom Mugeuk's shadow organization and direct subordinate unit. Although it now followed the Young Leader, it was also an organization that would grow incomparably once Geom Mugeuk became the Leader.

That position had ultimately been taken by Seo Jin, who had been Geom Mugeuk's closest friend. Fate was somehow continuing to tie their relationship together like this.

Seo Jin asked carefully.

"Will you be alright, Team Leader?"

"I am not alright. How could I be alright when I'll be in the position of a returned prodigal?"

But Cheongmyeon's eyes behind the mask were smiling.

"Still, I don't regret the days I spent here."

Seo Jin felt regret. If he had known they would part like this, he would have talked much more with him.

"Let's drink our fill at today's banquet."

"Let's do that, Leader Seo."

Meanwhile, the ceremony was now moving to the next order.

"Now, next will be the wedding officiant."

The Pungcheon Leader, who had stayed up all night practicing the officiant speech, took a deep breath and walked out from behind the folding screen with the eyes of someone entering a battlefield.

'I can't forget what I memorized!'

The Pungcheon Leader walked out with the most dignified expression possible.

His eyes met with Sobaekta, who was sitting in the front row.

*My disciple, have you been well?*

*Master, have you been well?*

*As you can see, I am doing well.*

*I wished to see you.*

The Pungcheon Leader wanted to show Sobaekta how well he was living here and how important a person he was.

The Pungcheon Leader stood facing Geom Mugeuk and Ian.

All the guests' eyes were focused on him. Having lived his life as the Pungcheon Leader, he was not someone to be nervous under such attention.

The Pungcheon Leader looked at Geom Mugeuk and Ian. Though he was not their parent, this moment was incredibly moving.

Then, from far away between the two, the figure of Go Wol was visible in the guest seats. He was sitting among the guests, looking at him and smiling.

The moment he saw that smile, countless past events filled and rushed through his head, carrying away the officiant speech he had memorized all night.

'*Gasp!*'

He couldn't remember anything.

The Pungcheon Leader was bewildered. He had memorized it so thoroughly; how could he not remember? He felt as if he had been afflicted by some sorcery.

'*Say something, anything!*'

He had to improvise, but perhaps because his desire to do well was too great? His mind had already turned white like the walls of the Geugak Soma's room.

The Pungcheon Leader's face flushed bright red. He truly felt like hiding using the Space-Time Shift Technique.

Then, Geom Mugeuk's voice transmission flew in.

—*More than anyone else's congratulations, I want to receive the Leader's. Just a single word of congratulations will do.*

The moment he heard those words, the Pungcheon Leader's flustered heart calmly settled.

*Whew, to think I'm receiving your help even on a day like this!*

Yes, if it were someone else, maybe, but the other party was Geom Mugeuk. Why would he need some sweetly memorized officiant speech? Just looking at his face like this, there was so much to say.

Soon, the Pungcheon Leader opened his mouth.

"Today, I will conduct the officiant speech in a comfortable manner, speaking directly to the marrying couple. Guests, please understand."

Having asked for their understanding thus, the Pungcheon Leader spoke to the two.

"I'm truly glad that my first time officiating a wedding is for you two."

The Pungcheon Leader gazed at Geom Mugeuk.

"I thought you would never marry."

There was certainly good reason to think so.

"When you see an evil bastard, you can't just pass by. When you see a wronged person, you can't pass by. When you see someone stuck, you somehow try to help them through. When someone misunderstands, you clear the misunderstanding. When someone is bound in shackles, you free them. Where in the world do you have the leisure to get married?"

Among the guests, those who knew Geom Mugeuk all smiled faintly. Those words all applied to them.

Those who didn't know Geom Mugeuk could instantly understand what kind of person he was from those words.

Especially Yong Jinhu could understand why his father had tried to introduce the Young Leader to him. He could also understand why the Martial Alliance Leader had come here personally.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Ian, who stood beside him, and shouted.

"I liked her too much to hold back!"

Laughter burst from all around at Geom Mugeuk's shout. Ian's face turned red and she lowered her head.

As Geom Mugeuk lightened the mood, the Pungcheon Leader felt even more at ease.

"Among the countless promises a person can make, I believe the hardest promise to keep is the promise to protect another person for life."

The Pungcheon Leader's gaze turned toward Go Wol in the distance. The reason he had abandoned the position of Leader and come to the Young Leader was because of his heart wanting to protect Go Wol.

Go Wol nodded with eyes that said he knew all of his feelings.

The Pungcheon Leader's gaze, which had been on Go Wol, turned back to Geom Mugeuk.

"You are the person I know who can keep that promise best. How about it? Can you protect your heart for a lifetime?"

"Yes! My heart will beat with her forever."

This time, the Pungcheon Leader looked at Ian.

"Your man is one who meddles in all the world's affairs. If a beggar blocks the road crying, I would kick him aside, but your man is someone who would crouch down beside him. You know this and are marrying him, right?"

At the Pungcheon Leader's question, Ian answered in a low voice.

"Yes."

"Then that's enough."

The Pungcheon Leader smiled brightly and added.

"Be happy."

With that, the officiant speech ended. Applause and cheers burst from all around.

As he came down from the platform, Sobaekta bowed his head in respect.

*Master, you were magnificent!*

*You rascal, who do you think I am? All the Central Plains bastards are beneath me!*

The Pungcheon Leader's back was drenched in sweat.

The next part of the ceremony continued.

Jo Chunbae and his wife watched the proceedings at ease. Normally, sitting this far forward would have made them uncomfortable, but Jang Ho and Seo Daeryong’s families were seated on either side of them.

Beside them was Gang Dal, who had gone from head of the Jeongan Branch to Third Executive Captain of Hwangcheon Pavilion. Clever and diligent in everything he did, he was already being evaluated as someone who, at this rate, might rise all the way to Chief Executive Captain.

Near them, Chungui and Maui were discussing medicine.

Geom Mugeuk had arranged their seats side by side so that the two could talk about the healing arts.

Having heard in advance that she would be coming, Maui greeted her first, and afterward the two continued conversing about medicine.

Chungui’s back was straight, and she no longer rubbed her limbs because they ached. Maui, too, had completely freed herself from the wounds of the past, and her face had brightened as if she had become an entirely different person.

Pyo Gigwang, Chungui’s disciple, had never seen his master so excitedly talking about medicine before.

Pyo Gigwang’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk. To think that he would show such consideration for Chungui even on the day of his own wedding—he could only surrender completely.

Beside them sat the Master of Cheonhwaru. Her emotion was unlike that of anyone else. This was the moment the fate she had glimpsed in advance became reality.

And the one who had shown it to her had not been Geom Mugeuk, but Ian. The reason Ian had told her she was special was because she had foreseen this very fate.

Next to her sat Goeak, a master of the unorthodox factions. Goeak had once helped Geom Mugeuk and left, and though he might ignore other matters, he had attended with the thought that he had to come to Geom Mugeuk’s wedding, at least.

In his entire life, he had rarely attended anyone else’s wedding, and this was the first time he had ever sat so quietly through one.

In the row behind them were Craftsman Gwak of the iron workshop, and Gwak Yeong, an iron workshop master from the Murim Alliance. She had come at Geom Muyang’s invitation.

“Starting tomorrow, take part in forging the treasured sword.”

“Truly?”

Her talent was far too outstanding to have her make only iron swords simply because she had come from the Murim Alliance.

“Instead, I’ll be supervising you even more strictly.”

“Thank you.”

Gwak Yeong was overjoyed. She thought that perhaps she might be able to make a proper sword for the First Young Master. My friend, look forward to it.

In the meantime, the wedding ceremony came to an end.

“The bride and groom shall now greet the guests.”

Geom Mugeuk and Ian bowed deeply and politely.

“Thank you!”

After greeting them in a loud voice, Geom Mugeuk looked out over the guests who had come to congratulate him. All the people who had died before his regression were alive.

They were looking at him with smiles. Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly back at them. They would think he was smiling because he was happy to be married, but this smile held a different meaning.

The place Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to last was Ian.

The moment their eyes met,

the two naturally moved toward one another and kissed. In that instant, the countless gazes watching them vanished from their awareness. In all the world, only the two of them remained.

Along with cheers erupting from every direction,

Bang! Baang! Baang! Bangbangbang!

Fireworks burst in the sky. Amid fireworks blooming with incomparable beauty,

“Now then, we will begin the celebratory banquet.”

Chapter 891: So, Whose Wedding Is It
------------------------------------
The wedding hall was transformed into a banquet hall.

The musicians took their places and began playing lively tunes in earnest.

The platform where the ceremony had been held was cleared away, and a stage was built there. Dancers climbed onto the stage and danced to the music.

Since they had been specially prepared for this day, they were dancers of the highest caliber.

All the chefs and kitchen workers of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult came out carrying tables laden with food and wine. The procession seemed endless.

Separate seats had been prepared for the Cult Leader, the Alliance Leader, the Palace Lord, and the experts who had accompanied them. A little farther away, places had been set up for the ordinary guests.

Enough food and wine had been prepared to fill the Great Training Hall, so everyone in attendance could enjoy the banquet.

Only then did Geom Ujin and Gwonma welcome Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, Han Seogyeong, and So Baekta.

Geom Ujin first greeted Jin Paecheon, for whom attending had likely been the greatest burden in many ways.

“Thank you for coming.”

“Of course I had to come.”

The fact that he had arrived with only a single carriage was enough to suggest how difficult the journey had been for him.

“Congratulations, Cult Leader Geom.”

“Thank you.”

“Congratulations, Venerable Gwonma.”

“Thank you, Alliance Leader.”

For today, at least, Jin Paecheon decided to forget Geom Ujin’s ambition to unite the martial world. He had truly come only to offer his sincere congratulations.

“Your son has gained a truly beautiful bride.”

“Her character is as outstanding as her appearance.”

“You are already bragging about your daughter-in-law?”

Geom Ujin smiled at Jin Paecheon’s joke.

Baek Jagang, who had been listening beside them, spoke with a laugh.

“A daughter-in-law like that is certainly worth bragging about.”

And so Baek Jagang naturally joined in the greetings as well.

“Congratulations.”

“Thank you for coming.”

He had brought the experts representing the Evil Alliance, filling five carriages with them.

Just as Jin Paecheon had shown the utmost respect by coming in a single carriage despite the political burden, Baek Jagang had shown the greatest respect possible in his own way.

So Baekta found it very unfamiliar to see the leaders of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions speaking so casually with one another. Judging solely by this scene, one could believe they were simply martial artists from the same organization having a conversation.

Han Seogyeong added a word to Geom Ujin’s praise of his daughter-in-law.

“As you all know, the bride is my niece.”

Geom Ujin raised the North Sea Ice Palace with a kind word.

“Where could the Ice Palace’s grace, clear and pure as snow, have gone?”

Geom Ujin was not ordinarily the sort of person to say things merely because they sounded pleasant to the other party, but today was an exception.

The family of Ian was involved, after all, and the North Sea Ice Palace had brought so many experts all the way here.

Han Seogyeong smiled at Geom Ujin’s words. She had been worried that Ian might be treated poorly because of her origins as a guard, but after coming here, she realized there had been no reason for such concern.

“Waaaaaaah!”

Just then, a roar rose from far away.

When Geom Mugeuk and Ian arrived there after changing clothes, people crowded around them. They had felt it during the wedding ceremony as well, but the cheers and congratulations were not merely formal. The people were shouting because they were genuinely happy.

“Don’t leave without meeting me first! You can’t just leave!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s cry, the Alliance Leader and the others, along with his friends and the Eight Venerables, all burst out laughing. It would take that chatterbox quite a while to meet every one of those people and make his way over here.

The first people Geom Mugeuk looked after were the residents of Maga Village, including Jo Chunbae.

“Thank you for coming.”

“Thank you so much for inviting us.”

All the residents of Maga Village had come to congratulate Geom Mugeuk.

It was the first time in their lives they had attended such a grand wedding, and the first time they had ever seen so many remarkable people gathered in one place.

They had already held Geom Mugeuk in profound respect, but being invited to an occasion like this made their feelings impossible to express.

They would probably talk about and boast of this wedding until the day they died.

“Come on, everyone. Come here and taste the dishes prepared by our cult’s chefs.”

Geom Mugeuk called them over to the tables. Otherwise, they would have been too conscious of the Demon Cult members to touch the food and wine. So an entire row of long tables had been set aside for the residents of Maga Village.

“Come on, let’s all have a drink!”

He poured wine for everyone and accepted a cup from Jo Chunbae.

“Congratulations on your marriage!”

After draining his cup, Geom Mugeuk made sure not to leave out a joke.

“Even if the dishes served here aren’t as delicious as our host’s cooking, you mustn’t let it show on your faces. You know why, don’t you?

The chefs are hiding somewhere and watching. If they see anyone making an unpleasant face after tasting the food—”

Geom Mugeuk put on a deliberately terrifying expression and drew a finger across his throat.

Everyone burst out laughing at the joke. It had been an awkward and frightening place for them, but Geom Mugeuk’s humor eased their tension.

“Now, enjoy your food while watching the performances. They say several performances have been prepared for today, so look forward to them.”

The residents of Maga Village watched the performances. It was their first time seeing such beautiful music and entertainment.

Jo Chunbae wiped away his tears.

“Why are you crying?”

“It’s nothing. I’ve never seen such an enchanting performance in my life.”

As if that were the real reason. He was crying again because he was grateful to Geom Mugeuk for taking such good care of them.

“Stay and watch all the performances before you leave.”

Geom Mugeuk took Jo Chunbae’s hand. Unlike on other days, when Jo Chunbae had been overwhelmed with gratitude, today he firmly grasped Geom Mugeuk’s hand in return.

Then, belatedly realizing what he had done, he exclaimed, “Oh!” and hurriedly withdrew his bony, warm hand in surprise.

The next person Geom Mugeuk and Ian looked after was Seo Daeryong.

“Young Cult Leader!”

“Pavilion Lord Hwangcheon.”

With countless eyes focused on him, Seo Daeryong behaved with dignity as he offered his congratulations. In his heart, he wanted to run over and embrace them tightly.

You know I’m the senior in this field, right? He wanted to congratulate them while putting on all sorts of airs and cracking jokes.

“Congratulations, Young Cult Leader.”

“Thank you.”

Beside Seo Daeryong stood his wife, Dana, holding their son.

Geom Mugeuk went over and took the child into his arms.

“Yeongha, your father is a truly wonderful man, someone the cult cannot do without. So you must grow up strong and healthy! Take after your mother in looks and your father in brains! Understand?”

Seo Daeryong had already been overwhelmed with emotion, but hearing Geom Mugeuk say such things in front of everyone moved him deeply.

Seo Daeryong’s sisters-in-law, Danbi and Danyeon, also offered their congratulations.

“Congratulations, Young Cult Leader!”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the youngest, Danyeon.

“At least today, I’m more handsome than my father, aren’t I?”

When they had first met, Danyeon had said that Geom Ujin was the most handsome.

Danyeon looked toward Geom Ujin in the distance. Her eyes were instantly drawn toward him, and that expression answered the question for her.

“That’s too cruel, little sister-in-law.”

Danyeon smiled and replied,

“Today, our Young Cult Leader is the most handsome!”

Jang Ho, who was standing beside them, cupped his fists politely and offered his congratulations.

“Many congratulations.”

Ian sent him a look. We absolutely have to hold our drinking gathering!

Jang Ho smiled and nodded.

Of course, it would be difficult in reality. Ian, the commander of the Ghost Guard, and Ian, the Young Cult Leader’s wife, would be living completely different lives.

“Lord Jang, be sure to paint our wedding ceremony today.”

“I understand.”

Next, Geom Mugeuk and Ian met Ak Gunhak and Cha Iran.

Cha Iran spoke to them.

“Thank you.”

Instead of saying congratulations, she said thank you.

Quick-witted as ever, Ian immediately understood why she had said it. Their marriage had evidently brought about a decisive change in the relationship between the two.

So Ian was the one who offered Cha Iran congratulations.

“Congratulations!”

“It’s all thanks to you.”

Did Geom Mugeuk really not understand what those words meant?

He whispered into Ak Gunhak’s ear.

“Is it all right to make a decision this suddenly?”

Ak Gunhak whispered back.

“Who do you think turned the man who wanted to quietly run an inn by himself into this?”

“I didn’t think you’d actually do it just because I told you to.”

Is this guy insane? Ak Gunhak was wondering whether he should grab the groom by the collar at least once, when—

“Stop teasing my man.”

The person who said it with such force was Cha Iran.

Geom Mugeuk whispered to Ak Gunhak again.

“I think it’s too late to turn back now.”

“I can hear you!”

At Cha Iran’s shout, Geom Mugeuk beamed. If the two of them ran the inn as husband and wife—

“A legendary inn will be born in the martial world.”

That was exactly what Cha Iran intended, now that they had started down this path.

“Yes. We intend to raise it into something like that.”

Ak Gunhak said to Geom Mugeuk,

“There are many people waiting. Go on and see them. Come visit us often.”

“Brother Ak, thanks for coming.”

Seeing Cha Iran link arms with Ak Gunhak, Geom Mugeuk and Ian turned away.

Just then, the dancers’ performance came to an end.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The sweet music stopped, and drums rang out for the next performance—the sword dance.

It felt as though the sound was expressing Ak Gunhak’s future.

“What are we going to do about our poor Brother Ak?”

Cha Iran’s voice carried from far away.

“I can hear you!”

Laughing, Geom Mugeuk and Ian hurried off.

On a day like this, they could have pretended to be busy and simply passed by.

But Geom Mugeuk made sure to greet every person who caught his eye.

“Chungui!”

When he called her name loudly, Chungui, who had been speaking with Ma Ui, was startled.

At the name Chungui, the people around them turned their eyes toward her.

Chungui? The money-grubber Chungui? That’s the money-grubber Chungui? Whispers arose here and there.

Geom Mugeuk infused his voice with inner power and spoke to everyone.

“You have all misunderstood her until now. Elder Chungui has spent her entire life raising orphans with the money she earned.

She has lived a more valuable life than anyone here. In the name of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Young Cult Leader, I guarantee that what I have said is true.”

When Geom Mugeuk revealed the truth about her, Chungui asked in surprise,

“Why are you doing this?”

She would normally have shouted angrily, but given the occasion, Chungui asked as politely as she could.

“You’ve been cursed at your whole life, haven’t you? I want you to spend the rest of your life being praised by people.”

“!”

“You deserve that much, Elder Chungui.”

“I did not do those things seeking such praise.”

“I know. That’s why I revealed it. Because I know you would never reveal it yourself.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at her gently as he spoke.

“I’ve already caused the trouble, so I don’t know anything!”

Geom Mugeuk led Ian away as though fleeing and added,

“Starting today, I’m off duty, and you’re off duty too, Elder Chungui!”

How could Chungui not understand Geom Mugeuk’s heart?

Flustered by the eyes pouring down upon her, she sighed. Then Ma Ui, who was standing beside her, spoke.

“To confess, I was the same.”

At her questioning look, Ma Ui told her about his own circumstances.

“I was living a different life from the one I live now. It was a dark and gloomy existence. But the Young Cult Leader dragged me out into a place where there was light.”

Ma Ui’s expression was peaceful, as if to show that he was not lying.

“I am satisfied with the life I live now.”

Ma Ui looked at her and added one last thing.

“How much longer do you think we have to live, anyway? That remarkable young man led us into the light, so let’s pretend we can’t resist and live this life as our own. We can meet from time to time and talk about medicine as well.”

Chungui eventually nodded. Yes, her life had already changed greatly because of the Young Cult Leader. Fine. Let’s see what life in this bright world will be like.

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk continued on and this time went to see the Lord of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

The Lord of Cheonhwa Pavilion gave Ian a tight hug.

“This woman will live better than anyone.”

“You know, don’t you? I believe everything you say as though it were carved in stone.”

“Yes. You may believe me.”

“Thank you.”

As the two women spoke, a person walking in the distance caught Geom Mugeuk’s eye.

He was as small as an ant from here, but Geom Mugeuk could tell that it was Goeak.

—Thank you for coming.

Goeak was startled. He was surprised that Geom Mugeuk had recognized him from so far away, and even more surprised that he had sent a voice transmission.

It was clear that Geom Mugeuk had reached the realm where he could freely employ the Thousand-Li Voice Transmission technique.

Goeak could not send his voice that far, so he merely waved.

“Live well, Young Cult Leader.”

He had been walking away, thinking that he would never see Geom Mugeuk again until the day he died. But after exchanging such a final farewell, he did not feel bad.

While Geom Mugeuk and Ian greeted the guests, Geom Ujin was drinking with the Alliance Leaders, Han Seogyeong, and So Baekta.

They talked not only about the Central Plains, but also about the state of affairs in the Outer Lands and the Northern Sea.

It was truly as if the entire martial world had gathered in one place for a conference.

A little farther away, the young successors were seated, while the Eight Demon Venerables, the Evil Dao Alliance, and the experts from the Northern Sea and the Outer Lands sat in separate groups beside them, drinking.

As they talked, a cheer rose from the direction where Geom Mugeuk was standing. The Faceless Guests had come to congratulate Geom Mugeuk and Ian.

Amid the Faceless Guests in their splendid masks, Geom Mugeuk was laughing with delight. It was such a radiant smile that one could not help but wonder if anyone really could be that happy.

Not only the Faceless Guests, but also the Demon Swords, Blade Devils, and Iron Fists were mingling together. Normally, the subordinates of the Demon Lords did not associate with one another, but at least today, they mingled as though they belonged to a single organization.

Watching Geom Mugeuk surrounded by people, Jin Paecheon suddenly had a thought.

When had it been? When had he ever stood like that among the martial artists of the Martial Alliance? And when had he ever mingled with the residents near the Martial Alliance headquarters, the way he had with the people of Maga Village?

Had there truly ever been such a time?

At some point, he had become busy and begun living a life surrounded only by certain people. Among experts and venerable masters—though looking back now, it was not as if he had been quite that busy.

Why had he lived as though he were always being pursued?

As he let out a soft sigh, Baek Jagang silently poured him a drink, as though he knew exactly what Jin Paecheon was thinking.

Jin Hagun and Bisa-in also drank as they watched Geom Mugeuk.

“As always, that fellow gives us an answer.”

Jin Hagun believed that sight was the answer to what kind of Alliance Leader he ought to become.

Bisa-in gave a faint smile and said,

“The problem is that even if you peek at the answer sheet of the person beside you, it isn’t easy to write down the answer.”

Everyone smiled faintly at his words. What Geom Mugeuk was showing them now was not something one could accomplish merely by imitating him.

“Still, there is one fortunate thing.”

Han Seol spoke unexpectedly.

“The two of them will show us the answer there as well, while living their married life together.”

Jin Haryeong shook her head and replied,

“Though I cannot say for certain, I doubt even that answer will be easy to copy. Not if it is those two as husband and wife.”

Everyone wore expressions of agreement at her words.

“In any case, he is far too popular. It will take quite a while for him to make his way over to us.”

Bisa-in looked toward Geom Mugeuk. This time, the members of the merchant groups who had come as wedding guests were not letting him go.

Yong Jamyeong of the Silver River Merchant Group was introducing his son, while Geum A-su of the Golden Manor was presenting his grandson, who had grown so much.

Geom Mugeuk was jokingly telling them to line up according to the order in which they had brought their expensive wedding gifts.

The road from there to here was long—very long.

Even though he was a man whose qinggong was faster than anyone else’s, a man who could fly like a ray of light.

Yes, that was probably why.

Because he was the one who had filled that road to overflowing with people, because he knew how deep the feelings contained in each of those steps were.

That was why the Cult Leader, the Alliance Leader, the Palace Lord, and their successors were all so willing to wait.

Even when the Cult Leader, the Alliance Leader, and the Palace Lord were waiting, he was the sort of man who would first take the hands of the people of Maga Village. That was why they were willing to wait.

Bisa-in emptied the cup in his hand and said with a smile,

“Well, whose wedding is it? How could it end so easily?”

Chapter 892: It's a Good Day, Isn't It?
---------------------------------------
They had finally arrived.

Geom Mugeuk and Ian greeted their two fathers first, then bowed to the two Alliance Lords in turn.

Baek Jagang said to Geom Mugeuk with a smile,

"I was wondering why you hadn't come to taste my cooking. You had fallen in love."

"I believe you will have to prepare meals for two from now on."

"Of course I will. Come visit anytime."

Han Seogyeong looked at Ian and thought of her deceased older sister.

"Your mother would have been so happy to see you like this."

Ian looked up at the sky.

'Are you watching? Come to think of it, I've been so distracted today that I haven't even thought of Mother. Please forgive me.'

That was as far as her heart would go regarding her mother. She did not try to delve deeper. She stopped at the entrance because she knew that place was filled only with pain and longing. Because she believed her mother would have wanted it that way.

"The Ice Palace will always be open for you. Never forget."

"Yes, thank you, Aunt."

It was the first time she had heard the word "Aunt" directly from her, so Han Seogyeong's face brightened radiantly.

Watching Geom Mugeuk and Ian go to greet the Demon Lords, Geom Woojin said proudly,

"I was the first to marry off my son."

It was a boast brought up while recalling the day of the Three Faction Summit when they had bragged about their children. Today was a certain victory.

Jin Pacheon conceded defeat.

"It stings, but I have lost."

But Baek Jagang had no intention of conceding so easily.

"We may have lost in marriage, but who sees their grandchild first is yet to be seen."

Baek Jagang glanced at Bisa-in and Han Seol sitting before him.

He had heard in passing earlier that those two children had grown close lately. Jin Pacheon had naturally grown anxious, but he too had one final move.

"You may only see a daughter-in-law, but I can see both a granddaughter-in-law and a grandson-in-law."

At those words, Geom Woojin and Baek Jagang laughed.

So Baekta, who had been watching, couldn't believe it. The Demonic Cult Leader, the Martial Alliance Lord, and the Sa Do Alliance Lord were engaging in such childish competition!

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk and Ian went to where the Eight Demon Lords and other experts were seated. They first greeted the experts from outside respectfully, then went to the Eight Demon Lords.

"Elder!"

Geom Mugeuk went to Hyeolcheon Doma first. Even with the other Demon Lords beside him, the first person he always sought was Hyeolcheon Doma.

And yet, Hyeolcheon Doma wore a somewhat sulky expression for some reason.

"Why do you look like that?"

Then Ilhwa Sword Lord, who was beside him, quietly explained,

"He had been practicing officiating the wedding ceremony."

"When did I!"

It seemed he had expected to be asked to officiate. Geom Mugeuk consoled him.

"I thought you wouldn't want to do it again after having done it once as the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord. And think about it. Our broad-minded elder here would quickly let it go if I spoke to him like this, but the Wind Heaven Cult Leader would hold it against me for three years."

Just then, a chilly voice came from behind.

"Three years? Not thirty?"

When he turned around, the Wind Heaven Cult Leader was approaching.

"Now the tables have turned. You are talking behind my back, and I appear."

Geom Mugeuk laughed and placated him.

"Cult Leader! How could that be? I was joking because I knew you were coming. Please accept a drink from me."

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader accepted the drink from Geom Mugeuk.

"Thank you so much for today. I will live well, as you advised."

Ian also bowed respectfully alongside Geom Mugeuk.

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader emptied the cup Geom Mugeuk had given him. And now, he conveyed the sincerity he hadn't been able to express when officiating earlier.

"Don't tire yourself. I............ still need you in the future."

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader said such words without hesitation, even in a place with the Cult Leader, the Alliance Lords, and the Eight Demon Lords.

"Yes. I won't tire."

"That's good enough."

Geom Mugeuk received a cup of liquor from each of the Eight Demon Lords.

He received the first from Hyeolcheon Doma.

"We're going to read books together now."

"I suppose I should buy some new books."

Hyeolcheon Doma couldn't bring himself to tell him not to come because Ian was right beside him. Still, he said this much, since Ian was by his side now.

"I'll make sure to bring my nightclothes."

Ian poured liquor for Ilhwa Sword Lord.

"I ask for your continued guidance in the future."

Because Ilhwa Sword Lord was the most outstanding swordsman in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult after Geom Woojin and Geom Mugeuk.

Ilhwa Sword Lord's gaze toward Ian was different from before. Because she was now the wife of the Young Cult Leader.

"What is the Little Demon Empress's goal?"

Hearing herself called the Little Demon Empress, Ian started. It was a moment when the reality of her marriage truly sank in. After a brief pause, Ian answered calmly,

"To see the pinnacle of swordsmanship."

Ilhwa Sword Lord nodded. If their path was the same,

"We might walk it together."

Chwima poured liquor into the Ice Palace chalice for Geom Mugeuk. It was a formal occasion, so he did so respectfully.

"This is the liquor I prepared for today."

Geom Mugeuk drank with Ian.

"It's truly delicious."

"I brought out the finest liquor I possess."

He always said it was the best, but an even tastier liquor kept appearing.

"When I drink the liquor at Lord Chwima's wedding, I will reassess then."

The Demon Lords who heard this laughed.

Seopheon Majon watched Geom Mugeuk's life advancing step by step and looked forward to the day he would ascend to the seat of Cult Leader.

Ma Bul offered an unnecessary jab.

"The older brother hasn't even married yet; is it okay for the younger brother to marry first?"

However, a bright and clear radiance that appeared when he was in good spirits flowed from Ma Bul's body as he poured the liquor.

Poison King spoke to Ian.

"Little Demon Empress, stop by the Cheon Dokrim when you have time and help me with my work."

Though he said help, he meant to teach her about poisons.

"Yes, I will definitely visit you."

The two walked to Geugeuk Soma last.

Geugeuk Soma took out two masks from his bosom and handed them over. They were the Hwanrak Demon Masks that he and the maskless guests were currently wearing.

"They are masks worn during festivals. I prepared them as a gift with the meaning that you should live your lives like a festival forever."

They had previously received ordinary masks as gifts, but this was their first Hwanrak Demon Mask.

"Yes, we will live our lives like a festival forever."

The two put on the masks and faced each other. Inside the masks, the three of them smiled brightly.

Just then, Geom Woojin rose from his seat and walked over to where the Demon Lords were.

Geom Mugeuk and Ian took off their masks and tucked them away.

Geom Woojin said to Chwima,

"Did you bring it?"

"Yes, Cult Leader."

Chwima brought a liquor bottle from behind.

"It is the spirit of Daechurim."

He couldn't imagine how much distress Chwima had suffered worrying that it might spoil. Yet today, the Cult Leader had ordered only one bottle of the spirit to be brought.

Geom Woojin took the spirit and poured it for the leaders as he spoke.

"This is the liquor that represents our cult."

Hearing those words, Chwima was moved. The Cult Leader had originally considered it a superstition to divine the peace of the Divine Cult based on whether the Daechurim spirit spoiled or not.

But today, the Cult Leader had called the spirit the liquor that represented the cult.

"Now, everyone raise your cups."

His low voice rang clearly in the ears of every guest in the great banquet hall.

Who would dare defy his words? Everyone filled their cups and raised them.

When Geom Woojin raised his cup, everyone stood and raised their cups.

"Thank you for attending my son's wedding. Let us toast."

This time, Geom Mugeuk was deeply moved. He had never expected his father to have the spirit brought personally and to propose a toast himself like this.

It was a moment that made him realize once again just how happy his father was today.

Everyone there emptied their cups. It was a moment of drinking with the Heavenly Demon. For the demonic people, it was a moment of tremendous glory.

Even for a non-demonic person, this was a moment one could never forget for a lifetime.

After the toast, Geom Woojin returned to his seat and conversed with the Alliance Lords.

'Thank you, Father.'

Geom Mugeuk went to Geom Muyang, who was standing a short distance away.

"Hyung, let's have a drink together."

"I'm fine."

"Did you forget? You are a full-fledged member of our friend group too."

Geom Mugeuk took Geom Muyang to where his friends were.

"Did you wait long?"

Geom Mugeuk emptied his cup with his brother and friends.

Geom Mugeuk hadn't expelled the intoxication with internal energy, so his face was flushed. For today, he had decided simply to get drunk.

None of the friends there had expelled their intoxication either. Everyone was pleasantly drunk.

Jin Ha-gun spoke to the two of them.

"It was tough, wasn't it? Take it easy now."

He neither congratulated nor thanked them. Having said such things countless times to people until now, this meant they should rest, at least in this place.

They had originally planned to join forces with Bisa-in to tease Geom Mugeuk. They had intended to mock the youngest for getting married.

But seeing the two of them arrive exhausted, they didn't feel like it.

"Still, it's comfortable and nice being with friends."

Bisa-in asked Geom Mugeuk,

"Didn't you tell me before to have about seven children and line them up? How many do you plan to have?"

At the talk of children, Ian's face flushed slightly.

"Wouldn't one pretty daughter who resembles the young lady be enough?"

Then Ian said,

"We need one son who resembles you, too."

Then Jin Ha-gun shook his head and said,

"That would be a problem. If he has a son resembling that friend, our children will suffer."

Bisa-in sighed theatrically, resigning himself to the inevitable.

Han Seol offered a different possibility.

"Unexpectedly, a taciturn son quite unlike his father could be born."

Jin Ha-ryeong imagined such a son.

"The father keeps chattering away, and the little son listens maturely. Wouldn't that be a sight?"

Everyone laughed at her words.

They all drank together. Indeed, with Geom Mugeuk there, the gathering felt complete.

Then exciting music began to play from the stage. Dancers who had changed their clothes were dancing to the music.

If the piece played earlier had been elegant, this one was exciting.

Bisa-in quietly rose from his seat.

They thought he might formally offer congratulations, but what followed was an action that surprised everyone.

Bisa-in began to dance lightly to the music. He was someone who had vowed there would be no more dancing in his life.

No one had imagined Bisa-in would get up and dance while drinking, so everyone watched with surprised expressions.

"Did something happen during my wedding?"

Despite Geom Mugeuk's joke, Bisa-in gave himself to the music. He didn't care about the attention around him at all. He wasn't embarrassed.

"It's a good day, isn't it?"

It was neither a practiced dance nor a grand one. It was simply a dance of joy.

This dance was his congratulatory performance for Geom Mugeuk.

Once again today, he showed Geom Mugeuk by example what kind of Alliance Lord he should become. He couldn't live exactly like that, but at least he could see the direction. Because in life, direction matters more than speed.

'Congratulations, friend, and thank you.'

Han Seol, who had been watching Bisa-in dance, rose from her seat.

"Are you going to sing?"

Geom Mugeuk had asked that because she had sung while dancing in the past.

"No, I want to dance too."

Han Seol began to sway lightly and dance beside Bisa-in.

Geom Mugeuk was startled.

"Those two have definitely been bitten by a venomous insect that causes madness, spending so much time together lately."

But there was another person bitten by that venomous insect. Jin Ha-ryeong stood up.

"I can't be left out when it comes to dancing."

"What's with you, too?"

"None of our alliance's experts came today."

Meaning there was no reason to be embarrassed.

Jin Ha-ryeong began to sway gently beside the two of them.

Of course, the Cult Leader and the Alliance Lords, the Demon Lords and the experts all watched them dance. Since it wasn't the first time, no one was surprised anymore.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Ha-gun.

"Your sister is starting to go mad too."

Just then, Jin Ha-gun slowly rose from his seat.

"Why so serious? Ha-gun, come to your senses. You're not like this."

Of course, Jin Ha-gun wasn't like this. He hadn't been, yet he had become someone who would say such things.

"Didn't you all put on a congratulatory performance when I became the Young Alliance Lord? Today is your wedding day; there's no reason I can't join in."

Jin Ha-gun began to dance too.

Not all of them were dancing with big movements. Everyone swayed gently, pleasantly.

Geom Mugeuk furtively glanced at his older brother.

“Want to die?”

At his brother’s single remark, Geom Mugeuk laughed.

“Right, so not everyone was bitten by a venomous bug.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Ian.

At the look in his eyes that said, Let’s go too! Ian looked toward Geom Ujin.

Father is watching.

So what? He’s watching his son and daughter-in-law dance.

Her hesitation did not last long. Since Bisa-in had danced without even being told to, and all their friends had risen, how could they remain seated?

Let’s go.

Let’s go! If you’re truly embarrassed, don’t we have masks?

Geom Mugeuk and Ian also rose and began dancing with them.

Since Ian was in front of Geom Ujin and the Fist Demon, she truly only swayed her upper body ever so slightly.

The ones who were truly shocked were the ordinary guests.

Their eyes widened in astonishment. The successor of the Divine Cult, the successor of the Murim Alliance, the successor of the Evil Dao Alliance, and the successor of the Ice Palace were all dancing.

It was, quite literally, the entire future of the murim dancing.

There had never been a banquet like this in the history of the murim. It was not a formal banquet, but a festival where the bride and groom danced together with their friends.

Watching them dance, Yong Jamyeong said to his son,

“Son, whether you inherit the family business or do something else, whatever it is, work hard at it. It feels like a wonderful era is coming for you all.”

Geom Ujin, who was watching, wore an expression that said they were hopeless, but the corners of his mouth were smiling.

Baek Jagang’s small eyes gleamed as he watched the dancing youths.

“The Young Cult Leader makes my blood boil. He makes me want to return to my youth and mingle with him.”

Jin Paecheon, who was listening, felt much the same. Even if he returned to his younger days, with his personality he doubted he would dance with them, but at least he completely agreed with the part about his blood boiling.

The Eight Demon Venerables drank as they watched them dance. Seeing those young people dancing to the lively music stirred a passion in their hearts.

They drank while recalling their youth, now gone. The young Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable wanted to rush out.

Geom Mugeuk shouted to the guests,

“Anyone who wants to dance, let’s all dance!”

At those words, Sipchilgyo rose from her seat as if she had been waiting.

“I want to dance too.”

At Sipchilgyo’s words, the Crescent Moon Faceless Guest sprang to his feet.

“Let’s dance!”

This was what made masks so nice. The two began dancing without hesitation, and the Faceless Guests around them began circling them in a bizarre dance. Since they were people who did bizarre things on a regular basis, the sight did not seem strange at all. Only now did they truly seem like Faceless Guests.

Jo Chunbae looked at his wife and was smacked on the back.

“Don’t be ridiculous!”

Everyone enjoyed this exhilarating banquet.

Because they all instinctively knew it would happen only once in a lifetime, their hips swayed to the exciting music.

Amid the gradually rising heat, Geom Mugeuk shouted to the musicians,

“Play something even more exciting!”

Chapter 893: Drive the Carriage Slowly
--------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk was sitting on the bed, looking at the painting on the wall.

It was a painting of his and Ian’s wedding ceremony. It showed the two of them in ceremonial robes, holding their wedding, with his father and master beside them.

There was not just one painting. Beneath it were smaller ones: scenes of them dancing with their friends,

parting with those friends the next day, and setting out on their bridal journey.

They were the paintings Jang Ho had drawn and given to him as a gift. Not only the events of that day, but even what happened afterward had been rendered into splendid paintings and presented to him.

Just then, someone behind Geom Mugeuk wrapped an arm around his neck like an assassin.

“To think the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult would be so full of openings!”
